# BRODY_PHASE12E0_A_STRUCTURAL_NO_MATERIAL_PROOF_AUDIT_20260527

Status: DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY

## Scope
Audit existing proof that Brody can answer by structure/coherence without requiring memory material.

## Important distinction
- Memory material is enrichment.
- Memory material must not become a hard dependency for Brody response.
- Structural coherence must remain available when memory_response_chain has NO_MATERIAL or NO_MEMORY_RESULTS.
